26:["$","p","p-13",{"children":["I want my game to run on Android for now, and for that, I will use ",["$","a","a-0",{"href":"https://cordova.apache.org/","rel":"noreferrer","target":"_blank","children":"Cordova"}],". First I need to install Cordova with ",["$","code","code-0",{"children":"npm install -g cordova"}],", then ",["$","a","a-1",{"href":"https://cordova.apache.org/docs/en/10.x/guide/platforms/android/index.html","rel":"noreferrer","target":"_blank","children":"install Android Studio and yada yada"}]," and then finally create my project with ",["$","code","code-1",{"children":"cordova create skate"}],"."]}]
27:["$","p","p-14",{"children":["Cordova will expect the build files to be in a ",["$","code","code-0",{"children":"www/"}]," folder, that’s why I had to change my Webpack script before. Now I can simply run ",["$","code","code-1",{"children":"cordova build --release android"}]," and an unsigned APK file will be generated for me."]}]
